---
title: "kubernetes_apps_stateful_set"
description: "`kubernetes_apps_stateful_set` manages pods that need a real, stable identity -- a persistent name, a persistent network address..."
---

`kubernetes_apps_stateful_set` manages pods that need a real, stable identity -- a persistent name, a persistent network address, their own dedicated storage that follows them through a reschedule -- the real fit for databases and anything else that can't treat its own instances as interchangeable.

## Example

<Tabs>
  <Tab title="Go">
    ```go
    package main

    import (
    	ubx "github.com/ubiquex/ubx-sdk-go/runtime"
    	apps "github.com/ubiquex/ubx-sdk-kubernetes/sdk/go/kubernetes/apps"
    )

    func main() {
    	ubx.Main(ubx.Stack("example", func() {
    		ubx.Intent(ubx.IntentInfo{Summary: "example own stateful set"})

    		ubx.Resource(apps.StatefulSet, "example", apps.StatefulSetConfig{
    			Name:      "example",
    			Namespace: "example",
    			Spec:      map[string]any{"selector": map[string]any{"match_expressions": []map[string]any{{"key": "example"}}}},
    		})
    	}))
    }
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="TypeScript">
    ```typescript
    import { intent, resource, stack } from "@ubx/sdk";
    import { StatefulSet } from "@ubx/sdk-kubernetes/kubernetes/apps/stateful_set";

    export default stack("example", () => {
      intent({ summary: "example own stateful set" });

      resource(StatefulSet, "example", {
        name: "example",
        namespace: "example",
        spec: { selector: { matchExpressions: [{ key: "example" }] } },
      });
    });
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="Python">
    ```python
    import ubx_sdk as ubx
    from ubx.kubernetes.apps.stateful_set import StatefulSet, StatefulSetConfig

    def describe():
        ubx.intent("example own stateful set")

        ubx.resource(StatefulSet, "example", StatefulSetConfig(
            name="example",
            namespace="example",
            spec={"selector": {"match_expressions": [{"key": "example"}]}},
        ))

    if __name__ == "__main__":
        ubx.run("example", describe)
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="Markdown">
    ```
    Create kubernetes_apps_stateful_set called "example", with
    namespace example, spec a set value.
    ```
  </Tab>
</Tabs>

<ResponseField name="spec" type="object" required>
  A StatefulSetSpec is the specification of a StatefulSet. Required.
  <Expandable title="properties">
    <ResponseField name="min_ready_seconds" type="number">
      Minimum number of seconds for which a newly created pod should be ready without any of its container crashing for it to be considered available. Defaults to 0 (pod will be considered available as soon as it is ready) Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="ordinals" type="object">
      StatefulSetOrdinals describes the policy used for replica ordinal assignment in this StatefulSet. Optional.
      <Expandable title="properties">
        <ResponseField name="start" type="number">
          start is the number representing the first replica's index. It may be used to number replicas from an alternate index (eg: 1-indexed) over the default 0-indexed names, or to orchestrate progressive movement of replicas from one StatefulSet to another. If set, replica indices will be in the range: [.spec.ordinals.start, .spec.ordinals.start + .spec.replicas). If unset, defaults to 0. Replica indices will be in the range: [0, .spec.replicas). Optional.
        </ResponseField>
      </Expandable>
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="persistent_volume_claim_retention_policy" type="object">
      StatefulSetPersistentVolumeClaimRetentionPolicy describes the policy used for PVCs created from the StatefulSet VolumeClaimTemplates. Optional.
      <Expandable title="properties">
        <ResponseField name="when_deleted" type="string">
          WhenDeleted specifies what happens to PVCs created from StatefulSet VolumeClaimTemplates when the StatefulSet is deleted. The default policy of `Retain` causes PVCs to not be affected by StatefulSet deletion. The `Delete` policy causes those PVCs to be deleted. Optional.
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="when_scaled" type="string">
          WhenScaled specifies what happens to PVCs created from StatefulSet VolumeClaimTemplates when the StatefulSet is scaled down. The default policy of `Retain` causes PVCs to not be affected by a scaledown. The `Delete` policy causes the associated PVCs for any excess pods above the replica count to be deleted. Optional.
        </ResponseField>
      </Expandable>
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="pod_management_policy" type="string">
      podManagementPolicy controls how pods are created during initial scale up, when replacing pods on nodes, or when scaling down. The default policy is `OrderedReady`, where pods are created in increasing order (pod-0, then pod-1, etc) and the controller will wait until each pod is ready before continuing. When scaling down, the pods are removed in the opposite order. The alternative policy is `Parallel` which will create pods in parallel to match the desired scale without waiting, and on scale down will delete all pods at once. Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="replicas" type="number">
      replicas is the desired number of replicas of the given Template. These are replicas in the sense that they are instantiations of the same Template, but individual replicas also have a consistent identity. If unspecified, defaults to 1. Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="revision_history_limit" type="number">
      revisionHistoryLimit is the maximum number of revisions that will be maintained in the StatefulSet's revision history. The revision history consists of all revisions not represented by a currently applied StatefulSetSpec version. The default value is 10. Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="selector" type="object" required>
      A label selector is a label query over a set of resources. The result of matchLabels and matchExpressions are ANDed. An empty label selector matches all objects. A null label selector matches no objects. Required.
      <Expandable title="properties">
        <ResponseField name="match_expressions" type="list(object)">
          matchExpressions is a list of label selector requirements. The requirements are ANDed. Optional.
          <Expandable title="properties">
            <ResponseField name="key" type="string">
              The key of the label that this match expression targets. In a Kubernetes label selector match expression, this field specifies the label key to match against. Optional; if omitted, computed by Kubernetes. **(AI-inferred)**
            </ResponseField>
            <ResponseField name="operator" type="string">
              The operator represents the relationship between the key and the set of values in a label selector requirement. Allowed values are 'In', 'NotIn', 'Exists', and 'DoesNotExist'. Optional; if omitted, computed by Kubernetes. **(AI-inferred)**
            </ResponseField>
            <ResponseField name="values" type="list(string)">
              The list of values that the label selector key must match when the operator is In or NotIn. This field must be empty when the operator is Exists or DoesNotExist. Optional; if omitted, computed by Kubernetes. **(AI-inferred)**
            </ResponseField>
          </Expandable>
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="match_labels" type="map(string)">
          matchLabels is a map of &#123;key,value&#125; pairs. A single &#123;key,value&#125; in the matchLabels map is equivalent to an element of matchExpressions, whose key field is "key", the operator is "In", and the values array contains only "value". The requirements are ANDed. Optional.
        </ResponseField>
      </Expandable>
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="service_name" type="string">
      serviceName is the name of the service that governs this StatefulSet. This service must exist before the StatefulSet, and is responsible for the network identity of the set. Pods get DNS/hostnames that follow the pattern: pod-specific-string.serviceName.default.svc.cluster.local where "pod-specific-string" is managed by the StatefulSet controller. Optional.
    </ResponseField>

        <ResponseField name="spec" type="object">
          PodSpec is a description of a pod. Optional.
          <Expandable title="properties">
            <ResponseField name="active_deadline_seconds" type="number">
              Optional duration in seconds the pod may be active on the node relative to StartTime before the system will actively try to mark it failed and kill associated containers. Value must be a positive integer. Optional.
            </ResponseField>
            <ResponseField name="affinity" type="object">
              Affinity is a group of affinity scheduling rules. Optional.
              <Expandable title="properties">
                <ResponseField name="node_affinity" type="object">
                  Node affinity is a group of node affinity scheduling rules. Optional.
                  <Expandable title="properties">
                    <ResponseField name="preferred_during_scheduling_ignored_during_execution" type="list(object)">
                      The scheduler will prefer to schedule pods to nodes that satisfy the affinity expressions specified by this field, but it may choose a node that violates one or more of the expressions. The node that is most preferred is the one with the greatest sum of weights, i.e. for each node that meets all of the scheduling requirements (resource request, requiredDuringScheduling affinity expressions, etc.), compute a sum by iterating through the elements of this field and adding "weight" to the sum if the node matches the corresponding matchExpressions; the node(s) with the highest sum are the most preferred. Optional.

<ResponseField name="status" type="object">
  StatefulSetStatus represents the current state of a StatefulSet. Optional; if omitted, computed by Kubernetes.
  <Expandable title="properties">
    <ResponseField name="available_replicas" type="number">
      Total number of available pods (ready for at least minReadySeconds) targeted by this statefulset. Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="collision_count" type="number">
      collisionCount is the count of hash collisions for the StatefulSet. The StatefulSet controller uses this field as a collision avoidance mechanism when it needs to create the name for the newest ControllerRevision. Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="conditions" type="list(object)">
      Represents the latest available observations of a statefulset's current state. Optional.
      <Expandable title="properties">
        <ResponseField name="last_transition_time" type="string">
          The last time the condition transitioned from one status to another. The timestamp is represented as a string in RFC3339 format. Optional; if omitted, computed by Kubernetes. **(AI-inferred)**
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="message" type="string">
          Human-readable message indicating details about the condition, such as the reason for the current condition status. Optional; if omitted, computed by Kubernetes. **(AI-inferred)**
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="reason" type="string">
          A machine-readable, CamelCase reason for the condition's last transition, providing a short identifier for why the condition is in the given status. Optional; if omitted, computed by Kubernetes. **(AI-inferred)**
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="status" type="string">
          The status of the condition, indicating whether the condition is True, False, or Unknown. Optional; if omitted, computed by Kubernetes. **(AI-inferred)**
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="type" type="string">
          The type of the StatefulSet condition, for example 'ReplicaFailure'. Optional; if omitted, computed by Kubernetes. **(AI-inferred)**
        </ResponseField>
      </Expandable>
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="current_replicas" type="number">
      currentReplicas is the number of Pods created by the StatefulSet controller from the StatefulSet version indicated by currentRevision. Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="current_revision" type="string">
      currentRevision, if not empty, indicates the version of the StatefulSet used to generate Pods in the sequence [0,currentReplicas). Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="observed_generation" type="number">
      observedGeneration is the most recent generation observed for this StatefulSet. It corresponds to the StatefulSet's generation, which is updated on mutation by the API Server. Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="ready_replicas" type="number">
      readyReplicas is the number of pods created for this StatefulSet with a Ready Condition. Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="replicas" type="number" required>
      replicas is the number of Pods created by the StatefulSet controller. Required.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="update_revision" type="string">
      updateRevision, if not empty, indicates the version of the StatefulSet used to generate Pods in the sequence [replicas-updatedReplicas,replicas) Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="updated_replicas" type="number">
      updatedReplicas is the number of Pods created by the StatefulSet controller from the StatefulSet version indicated by updateRevision. Optional.
    </ResponseField>
  </Expandable>
</ResponseField>
